# F14 — c3-safety-trust-local-first — safety-first autonomy and local-first, as built (main c36ae12e)

SUMMARY: The biggest cohesion problem is that the six-layer guardrail hierarchy in philosophy.md exists as code only on the secondary surfaces (proactive config proposals, HA service calls, MCP), while the primary surface the-being.md makes the core layer — the conversation — has exactly one guardrail: a regex risk classifier (ToolSafetyFramework) that decides between "execute now" and "ask first", with no dry-run, no diff, no backup, no audit log (ToolExecutor is built with audit_fn=None), no policy check, no budget, no cooling-off and no kill switch on that path. Probing the classifier on main shows the boundary is thin: `sed -i … /etc/ssh/sshd_config`, `dd if=/dev/zero of=/dev/nvme0n1`, `curl … | sh`, `zpool destroy`, `userdel -r`, `crontab -r`, `diskutil eraseDisk`, `launchctl unload -w` all classify MEDIUM (executed without confirmation, even for an unknown voice speaker), `iptables -F` and `ipset destroy` classify SAFE because the "network info" regex has no word boundary, and absolute home paths (`/home/x/.ssh/authorized_keys`) bypass the tilde-only sensitivity list. "Human approval" is real but fragmented across four surfaces (routes/approvals, routes/settings decide, /api/agent/confirm, MCP approve_proposal) with two of them not talking to the proposal pipeline; "kill switch" is a CWD-relative flag file read by four independent GuardrailEnforcer instances that only pauses scheduler jobs and non-critical notifications, with no UI to enter it; "budget limits" check resource usage after the job finished; "cooling-off" is a string suffix. The role/secure/sensitivity/capability gates that do exist compose coherently (each can only tighten), and the Tier 0/1/2 sensitivity model with the mcp_response choke point and the local-or-fail-closed secure turn are the strongest, best-tested parts of the trust model. Local-first mostly holds: no telemetry, no update checks, no analytics, cloud LLM providers only when the operator saves one — but the marketing sentence "web search is a switch, off by default" is false on the agent path: `web_search` is registered unconditionally, classified SAFE, and its handler queries DuckDuckGo with no enabled check, so the model can send query text off-box on any turn. Secrets: peer tokens are hashed and constant-time compared, the MCP static token is length-checked, but GET /api/llm/config still returns saved cloud api_keys in plaintext to an unauthenticated dashboard (R05-F3 open), and two of yesterday's "closed" security items (R1-F4 unlocked autonomy save, R2-P3 id-less tools/call executes) are still open on HEAD despite the security-review merge.

CONCEPT_MODEL: Halbert's trust model, as it should read now, has one owner and three concentric boundaries. The owner is the person at the keyboard of the canonical host: every dashboard route is unauthenticated and loopback-bound, so "reachable on the dashboard port" is treated as "admin" — and that assumption must be stated, enforced (refuse HALBERT_HOST off-loopback without a token) and never silently widened. The first boundary is what the model may DO: every side effect on the host, whatever surface it arrives from (chat, voice, MCP, peer, scheduler), passes through one gate — risk classification tightened by speaker role and skills, then a dry-run/diff for any file or config mutation, then human approval recorded in one approval ledger (ApprovalEngine) with an undo recorded next to the change, then an audit line. Autonomy level (observe/suggest/act/orchestrate) and the proactivity dial say how much of that chain may run unattended; safe mode is a single, XDG-persisted switch that every actor consults before acting, not only the scheduler. The second boundary is what the model may SEE and where it may THINK: Tier 0/1/2 sensitivity classifies config values, secure content latches the turn to a local model or fails closed, and Tier 2 crosses an egress point (mcp_response, compute endpoint) only with the human's typed acknowledgement — never to a cloud provider, never to a peer. The third boundary is what leaves the machine: nothing by default. Local inference, local index, no telemetry, no update checks; cloud LLMs, web search and peers are explicit operator switches, each off until enabled, each visible in Settings, and each surfaced to the model as unavailable rather than silently registered. Peers are bodies of the same entity — bearer-authenticated, hashed at rest, allowed only the read-only tool surface for compute, with memory and threads unredacted because they are internal — and an MCP client is an external caller, not a body: it gets the redaction choke point and the phrase gate, and its bearer token is the same credential system as peers, not a second one.

# F15 — t1-lexicon-sweep — terminology sweep against the founder-ruled lexicon (shell review §9.2) and the semantic-audit list; UI strings, prompts, CLI help, user-facing docs, marketing copy (main @ c36ae12e)

SUMMARY: The shipped shell (941cc14b + 601c2fdb) is lexicon-clean at the level that matters most: rail sections are Overview / Findings & Approvals / System / Workloads (Layout.tsx:81-111), the pill says "Singular Entity — one Halbert, many bodies" / "Independent Node" (PresencePill.tsx:163), the panel toggle says "Dashboard" / "conversation panel" (PanelToggle.tsx:39-56), Settings says "Identity & Voice" (Settings.tsx:79), and the marketing site (stops.jsx) contains zero banned terms and states the promise exactly ("I am not an assistant", "Linux today · macOS in beta", "Any LLM or BYOK"). The biggest cohesion problem is not a UI label at all: the production system prompt opens with `<role>Expert Linux/macOS system administrator assistant</role>` and "the sentient consciousness of this system" (config/prompts/v2/base/identity.xml:3-5) plus "You are a system administrator assistant" (safety.xml:5), because routes/agent.py:167 wires PromptBuilder as `base_builder` and agent_prompts.py:485-501 delegates to it — the philosophy-grade machine identity in `_get_identity()` (agent_prompts.py:66-80) is fallback-only. The model is literally told it is an assistant, contradicting philosophy.md and the marketing headline. The second-worst offender is README.md, the public face: "An AI assistant that actually knows your computer", "hybrid AI architecture: cloud models handle everyday conversation" (vs. marketing "cloud off by default"), "Fleet Mesh & Personalities", "MULTI-SESSION & FLEET ENGINE", "Auditory Cortex Engine", "Multi-Persona Store", "Host Mode / Home Mode" — five banned or superseded concepts in one file. Third: the word "assistant" is baked into every app-store/package description (tauri.conf.json:51-52, snap/arch/nix/flatpak packaging) and three in-app surfaces (About, Onboarding, Terminal). Genuine banned-in-UI strings that a user can see today are few and cheap: "Host Canvas" (TouchBar.tsx:50-51, in Voice Mode), "Halbert Auditory Cortex" and "offline/sovereign mode" (AudioSettings.tsx:165,479), "The being never initiates conversation" (BeingTab.tsx:403). The alias problems worth a decision are (a) the rail's "Findings" page actually renders security scanner discoveries (Findings.tsx:53 `api.getDiscoveries('security')`) while agent findings reach the UI only as a "Proactive Events" badge (ProactiveEventsBadge.tsx:86 via /api/being/events) — three nouns (finding / discovery / proactive event) for two concepts, and the page named for the concept shows the other one; (b) the device family — Settings says "Devices", the pill says "Linked Devices", the tab card says "Paired Bodies & Compute Providers", the compute card says "Workstation address", aria-labels say node_name — five nouns for one list; (c) "Chat" (19 strings, "Continue in Chat") vs. the ruled "Conversation"; (d) "persona" meaning the legacy it_admin/friend/custom switcher in the CLI (main.py:1911-1932) and README, versus BeingConfig/persona_id in the entity model. Docs lag the rulings as expected: FEATURES.md, dashboard-pages.md, ARCHITECTURE.md and macos-strategy.md still describe a sidebar with an "AI Assistant", a /chat route, session tabs that adopt "I AM <hostname>" (the code already leads with display_name, useHostIdentity.ts:11-14), and multi-instance/variant deployment. No backend renames are proposed anywhere below.

CONCEPT_MODEL: ## Canonical lexicon (extends shell review §9.2; UI label is what users read, code name is unchanged, aliases are banned or avoided in UI/docs/prompts/marketing)

| Concept | UI label | Code name (unchanged) | Banned / avoid in UI | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| The AI entity | **Halbert** (or the name chosen in onboarding, `display_name`) | `persona_id`, `BeingConfig`, `being.yml`, `routes/being.py`, `useBeingEvents` | "The Being", "Being", "Sovereign Host", "assistant", "chatbot", "AI persona", "sentient consciousness" | Prompts, About, Onboarding, packaging and README must not call Halbert an assistant; marketing headline is "I am not an assistant." |
| Entity config tab | **Identity & Voice** | `BeingTab.tsx`, `being_config.py` | "Being tab", "Self", "Personality" as the tab name | Shipped. Section header "Personality & Identity" is tolerable but "Identity" alone is cleaner. |
| Voice (self-reference) setting | **First Person / The Computer / Hybrid** | `voice: first_person|the_computer|hybrid` | — | Shipped (BeingTab.tsx:349-362); matches the-being §5. |
| Proactive dial | **Off / Quiet / Balanced / Assertive** | `proactivity` | "proactivity level" jargon in copy | Shipped; matches the-being §4. |
| Entity mode (shared) | **Singular Entity** | `entity_mode='singular'`, `canonical_memory_url` | "Unified Presence", "Unified mode", "Shared Presence", "One Halbert mode" | Tagline "one Halbert, many bodies" is fine as explanatory text. |
| Entity mode (separate) | **Independent Node** | `'independent'` | — | Only place "Node" is a UI word. |
| Physical device running Halbert | **Body** (its label is **Body Name**) | `body_name`; `node_id/node_name` (peers), `instance` (`/api/instance/info`) | "instance", "node", "host" (as a noun for the device), "machine tab", "satellite" | "This machine" / display_name for the current body. |
| The set of devices | **Linked Devices** | `federation/`, `peers_config.py`, `DevicesTab.tsx`, `/api/fleet/*`, `/api/peers/*` | "Federation", "Fleet", "Fleet Cockpit", "Compute Mesh", "Mesh", "Multi-instance", "Devices" (alone, as the tab) | Actions: "Link Another Device", "Remove". |
| Always-on device holding memory/threads | **Canonical Host** | `canonical_memory_url`, `canonical_thread_url` | "primary", "master", "hub" | Shipped (EntityIdentityCard.tsx:209-241). |
| Device offering LLM inference to another body | **Compute Peer** (field: "Compute peer address") | `ComputePeerCard.tsx`, `/api/compute`, `peerApi.ts` | "Workstation" (as the label), "Compute Provider", "offload node" | "workstation" stays valid as a *suggested body name* only. |
| Entity/body indicator in the top bar | **Presence Pill** (never shown as text; renders "Halbert @ desk") | `PresencePill.tsx` | "InstanceSwitch", "instance switcher", "host switcher" | Switching = "switch body" (singular) / "switch entity" (independent), §9.5. |
| The conversation surface | **Conversation** (panel) | `HostShell.tsx`, `AgentChat.tsx`, `ShellMode='engaged'`, `hostConversation.ts` | "Engaged mode", "Host Canvas", "Sovereign Host Shell", "Chat" as a surface name, "AI Assistant" | Buttons: "Continue in Conversation" / "Ask Halbert". |
| The dashboard surface | **Dashboard** (panel; also the `/` page) | `ShellMode='browsing'`, center panel | "Browsing mode", "system administration hub" | — |
| Topic threads (hidden continuity) | not surfaced; if ever, **Topic** | `continuity/` threads, `thread_id`, `CurrentTopicLabel` | "thread", "session", "conversation list" | Founder: one seamless chat, no conversation list. "Canonical thread URL" is an acceptable technical field. |
| One exchange | code only | `turn`, `turnId`, `Timeline.tsx` (the feed component) | "Timeline" as a UI noun | `home/timeline` is a different thing (event ledger). |
| Terminal session | **Terminal** / "terminal session" | `useTerminalSessions`, PTY session | "chat session", "agent session" in UI | Session is a UI word only for terminals. |
| Tool receipt | rendered as tool cards; code only | `render_recalled_receipts`, receipts | "receipt" in UI copy | — |
| Something needing human attention (agent-surfaced) | **Finding** / rail **Findings** | `findings/` `FindingStore`, `get_findings` | "alert" for this, "issue" as the noun, "discovery" | Today the /findings page shows security *discoveries* (see T1-11). |
| A change awaiting approval | **Approval** (queue: **Approvals** / "Approval Requests"); the item is a **Proposal** | `approval/`, `get_proposals`, `approve_proposal` | "pending action", "request" alone | Shipped. |
| Finding pushed into the conversation | fold into **Findings** (or "Halbert noticed"); today **Proactive Events** | `proactive/` `ProactiveEventBus`, `/api/being/events`, `useBeingEvents` | "Being events", "proactive channel" in UI | Decision needed (T1-11). |
| Threshold rule | **Alert Rules** (Settings) | `alerts/` | "alert" for findings | Keep "alert" strictly for threshold rules. |
| System fact from the scanners (the Eyes) | not a UI noun; pages show the facts (Services, Storage…) | `discovery/`, `/api/discovery`, `get_discoveries` | "Discoveries" as a page or badge name | mDNS "discovered peers" is a second sense → UI "Nearby devices". |
| Left rail | **Rail** | `NavRail.tsx` | "sidebar", "navigation" (as a name; a11y landmark label is fine) | Sections: Overview / Findings & Approvals / System / Workloads. |
| Center content | **page** | routes, `pages/` | "tab", "view", "module" in copy | Settings renders in the center panel. |
| Panels | **Dashboard panel** / **Conversation panel** | `PanelToggle.tsx`, `ShellModeContext` (`'engaged'|'browsing'|'both'|'voice'`) | "engaged/browsing" as labels | Code names stay per §9.2. |
| Right-hand evidence area in the conversation | **Context** | `ContextStage.tsx` | "stage", "context region", "drawer" | — |
| Domain module (dual-container) | design-only term **module** | `ModuleRenderer`, `modules/` | not used in UI copy | DOMAIN-MODULES doc concept. |
| What a body can do | **Capabilities** | `capabilities.py` `has_capability()`, `features` in `/api/instance/info` | "variant", "Host Mode / Home Mode", "sysadmin/home variant", "feature flag" | Variant survives in code as a *preset* only. |
| Knowledge base | **Knowledge** (tab), "Indexed Sources", "Search Indexes", "manuals" (marketing) | `rag/`, `corpus/`, `index/`, `knowledge/`, SourcePrep | "corpus", "RAG", "vector index", "scope" | Citations are "Docs" links. |
| Model assignment | **Model slot** (Guide / Specialist / Vision / Secure model) via **Models & Providers** | `roles`, `secure_model`, `config/prompts/v2/tiers/{guide,specialist,vision}.xml` | "Response tier", "Deep Thinker", "Eyes" (philosophy names), "role" | Philosophy's Guide/Deep Thinker/Eyes map to Guide/Specialist/scanners; UI keeps the shipped names. |
| Skills | not in UI (unwired) | `skills/`, ROLE-SCOPED-SKILLS | — | Do not surface until wired. |
| "Role" | avoid in UI | instance `role` (home/sysadmin), model role, message role | — | Overloaded three ways in code; never a UI word. |
| Smart-home surface | **Home** (rail item, `/home`) | `home/`, `routes/home.py`, `ha_connection` capability | "Ambient Home", "Space", "Overview & Space", "Zone" | "Home Assistant" only for the product; "Sentient Home" allowed in product/docs, not preferred in marketing (§9.1 #2); HA "Areas" only when mirroring HA. |
| Audio subsystem | **Audio** (tab "Audio & Voice") | `audio/`, `AcousticAuraIndicator.tsx` (name only) | "Auditory Cortex", "Acoustic Aura", "Somatosensory", "sovereign mode" | Settings copy: "Enable audio subsystem", "Wake word", "Speaker identification", "Wyoming" (protocol name, literal), "Wyoming satellite" (HA term for a mic device, never for a body). |
| Full-bleed voice route | **Voice Mode** | `/voice`, `ShellMode='voice'` | "kiosk" (future), "embodied presence" | Shipped. |
| Floating voice overlay | **Voice HUD** | `/voice-hud`, `VoiceHudSummonButton` | — | Shipped. |
| What Halbert remembers | **Memory**; the why is **rationale** ("I remember why you changed that") | `memory/`, `continuity/` (threads, receipts, recall), Haloysius `memory_v2`, `TemporalStateLedger`, `home/timeline` | "ledger", "autobiography", "continuity", "timeline", "biography" (prompt-only metaphor) | Marketing: "My logs are my memory." |
| Local-only model for sensitive turns | **Secure model** | `secure_model` slot | "secure layer" (README), "private model" | — |
| Config secret sensitivity | **Tier 1 — Operational Values / Tier 2 — Secrets & Credentials** under **Trust Boundary** | tier routing, redaction | "tier" for anything else (model slot, compression stages) | Deterministic template for Tier 2, never a model. |
| Autonomy | **Autonomy** (level, pause/resume, guardrails) | `autonomy/`, `autonomy.yml`, CLI `autonomy-*` | "safe-mode" as the noun | Matches philosophy hierarchy. |
| Unlock phrase | — | none | — | Zero occurrences anywhere; not a live term. |
| Health scans | **Health Checks** | scanners, `somatic/` (unwired) | "Somatosensory Loop", "REM Sleep", "Nightly Maintenance" | §9.2. |
